L02-commuting-repetition | Commuting-operator spectral threshold repetition
status=source_derived_unreviewed; type=curated_claim; source claims/derivations, not external certification.
Finite2-player game, commuting value v<1, answer-product d, minimum component resampling gap σ. ANY k≥1,0<δ<1−v: Pr[W_k≥ceil((v+δ)k)]≤exp[−σδ³k/(2048(1+log d))]. Arbitrary Hilbert spaces/commuting von Neumann algebras, not identification with finite tensor strategies.
Mechanism: separate scalar-ancilla amplification preserves each algebra; bounded forms/strong limits handle continuous spectrum at zero; mixed exposure+spectral barycenters remove minimum-atom path loss in release277. Half-erasure anchoring σ≥1/4 gives cubic anchored bound. Prior Lin anchored all-wins bound had gap power17; priority for wider threshold interface unestablished.
Dependencies: released277 finite-dimensional machinery plus complete local extension proof. Efficient question sampling DOES NOT imply usable spectral gap; succinct laws can have exponentially small gaps. No constant Hamiltonian locality, positive sparse enforcement, or quantumPCP conclusion. Next decisive application: named verifier with certified and charged question-chain gap; independent/formal proof review.

## Reading and checked scope

- All 13 allocated family catalogue entries were read. [FAMILY_ASSESSMENT.json](FAMILY_ASSESSMENT.json) records actual depth and deferrals.
- Family 175 was selected deepest: full reads of *Integral and fractional expectation thresholds are equivalent* (September 23), *Talagrand's discrete-convexity conjecture* (September 23), and *Graph Decompositions at the Integral Expectation Threshold* (October 5), including figures and references. Authors and dates were verified from exact pinned source. Source labels are not accepted as external validation.
- The load-bearing multiscale selector encoding was independently reconstructed: grouping by final coloring and the entire transition profile preserves one witness across all scales; maximal truncation plus one-level delay determines candidate moved vertices; an entropy/likelihood sum bounds the bad-color mass. The fractional rounding implication was checked with empty-set mass retained.
- The discrete-convexity signed kernel, product Fourier estimate, pair-sampling cover and exact row coupling were reconstructed and adapted coordinatewise. Saturated coordinates are removed by a section argument, rather than inserted into a Fourier basis with zero denominators.
- The graph-decomposition proof was read in full. Its useful requirement compression lemma controls all Hall tests by first imposing concentration for every small compressed family. Shared-vertex consistency among separate piece embeddings is absent from the theorem.
- Family 174 was selected second but retained at excerpt depth. The full thin-and-cheap outer reduction, Corollary8.1, was reconstructed **conditional on** the unverified core thin-tree algorithm. Full hierarchy geometry and signing/finite-bit construction were not audited by this worker. The source's nonnegative-cost output does not imply exact marginals.

[SOURCE_MECHANISMS_AND_LIMITS.md](../../../notes/frontier_combin2/cycle19/SOURCE_MECHANISMS_AND_LIMITS.md) provides exact interfaces, independent proof reconstruction, assumptions, limits, and the next gates.

W-frontier-combin3 | frontier combin3
status=reported_scope_in_extract; type=report_extract; source claims/derivations, not external certification.
SELECTED REPORT EXTRACT; not a complete theorem/proof. Omitted sections remain in the full source.

## Strongest consequence: a finite-relational-signature influence theorem

[Complete proof and boundaries](../../../notes/frontier_combin3/cycle19/RELATIONAL_INFLUENCE_AND_NOISE.md).

The family186 method extends to a fixed finite signature of q positive-arity relations, maximum arity r>=2, under independent common Bernoulli(p) coordinates and full vertex relabeling. Ordered tuples, repeats/loops and unary labels are allowed. Boolean invariant properties satisfy

`Var_p(f)<=C_{r,q} I_p(f)/(log n)^(r/(r-1))`,

with `C_{r,q}=O_r(q^(1/(r-1)))` apart from a finite small-n term. Increasing properties inherit the corresponding quantile-width bound. This is our internally reconstructed deduction from the source's restriction mechanism, not a theorem explicitly read in the release.

The new step is exact: a Fourier support of s relation coordinates uses v vertices with `s<=q v^r` and at most rs incidences, hence has a positive-degree vertex of degree at most `r q^(1/r)s^((r-1)/r)`. Conditioning outside a random vertex block preserves large coordinate orbits; capturing that one vertex transfers restricted logarithmic Fourier control to original degree `(sigma log n)^(r/(r-1))`. The common-bias cancellation is preserved.

A quantitative resampling consequence is

`Cov(f(X),f(Y))<=2r m^(-1/4)I_p(f)+exp(-tL)Var_p(f)`,

where `L=(sigma log m/(64 r q^(1/r)))^(r/(r-1))`, the block capture condition is required, and Y resamples each coordinate with probability t. In particular, if `I/Var=O(n^beta)` for `beta<a/4`, `m=floor(n^a)`, and `t(sigma log n)^(r/(r-1))->infinity`, normalized covariance vanishes.

Adversarial gates: a nullary bit disproves the extended statement if nullary symbols are admitted; a distinguished coordinate disproves it without full vertex invariance; linear real-valued coordinate sums disprove a squared-gradient spectral-gap interpretation. One-hot colors and arbitrary unequal biases require additional work. Sparse constraint thresholds with p about `n^(-(r-1))` can fall outside the useful noise regime and make the absolute width bound weak. No influence-estimation or threshold-finding algorithm has been acquired.

Exact finite identities were independently checked by direct configuration-pair enumeration and Fourier evaluation, including 512 directed-loop and64 mixed unary/binary configurations. [Evidence](influence_identity_evidence.json), [code](../../../work/frontier_combin3/cycle19/influence_identity_checks.py). These checks validate conventions and identities, not an asymptotic universal theorem.

# Cycle 19 design bridge: a sharp obstruction, conditional cube rounding

Worker: frontier_design_bridge; 7 October 2026. Release commit:
`adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a`.

The scoped investigation is complete. It does not establish a foundational
breakthrough or certify an upstream release theorem. It produces an
unconditional experiment-design obstruction with a sharp resource-relaxation
curve, and reconstructs the output conversions that are valid from the
examined sources. The positive cube conversion predates this release in
its mathematical ingredients.



## Strongest unconditional result

On the even cycle `C_(2r)`, exact degree one at every vertex is the common-base
constraint for two partition matroids. Its only feasible allocations are the
two alternating perfect matchings. The target `y=(1/2,...,1/2)` forces their
fair mixture. Feasible counting is exactly two; an exact uniform sample costs
one fair bit. Granting these ideal services cannot improve the following
output barrier.

Give even edges information contribution `diag(1,0)` and odd edges
`diag(0,1)`. These are positive rank-one matrices of norm one. The fractional
information is `F=(r/2)I`, and `V=sum H_e^2=rI`. Every feasible integral
allocation is singular and has operator error `r/2`. Its error divided by
`sqrt(||V||)` grows as `sqrt(r)/2`, even in matrix order two. Thus a universal
dimension-independent rank-one spectral rounding guarantee for arbitrary
two-partition common bases is impossible.

Retain exact marginals and hard cardinality `r`, and write `q` for the
probability that all degree budgets are exact. For
`Z=sum (-1)^e b_e`, every such law satisfies the sharp bounds

`Var Z >= q r^2+(1-q)(r mod 2)`,

`E ||sum b_e H_e-F||^2 >= [q r^2+(1-q)(r mod 2)]/4`.

A four-atom law attains both bounds for every rational `q`. Its two repair
atoms each have exactly one degree-zero and one degree-two resource, which
is the minimum total degree-error cost of two. At `q=0`, the information
matrix has minimum eigenvalue `floor(r/2)` and hard error zero for even `r`,
or `1/2` for odd `r`. Any hard nonsingularity promise forces `q=0` in this
instance. This gives a concrete protocol-admission test and an explicit
budget relaxation. Alternating-cycle geometry is classical; historical
priority of this exact Pareto formulation is unestablished.

I read the full constructive thin-tree manuscript, including Appendix A,
and the full Ezeunala--Jiang manuscript. Full reading is not full theorem
certification: the thin-tree paper imports a hierarchy companion and the
finite-bit signing algorithm has not been implemented here. The other
assigned papers have honest excerpt or abstract depth in
`FAMILY_ASSESSMENT.json`. Metric_acquisition independently examines 114's
counting mechanism; frontier_combin2 examines 175's complete proofs. Their
checks do not count as independent external validation.

All three Lean scope documents were fetched and Git-blob/SHA256 verified.
Doc 114 names common matroid bases, not the October binary-capacity
extension. Doc 174 describes the existence and deterministic binary-input
interfaces. Doc 175 describes threshold comparison and common-p discrete
convexity, without naming the October graph-decomposition extension.
No Lean source audit or build was run.

The exact Python fixtures pass for all 17,574 fixed-cardinality cycle
allocations with `r=2,...,8`, all information-matrix identities, five
rational points of each sharp curve, and 729 rational signed-cost
responses. They also check a small dyadic exact-mean matrix law and its
support compression, an equally informed independent-law bad-support
control, and the arbitrary-rank operator counterexample. The matrix
fixture uses exhaustive small signing, not an upstream source algorithm.
The universal proofs are in the note; finite tests have their stated scope.

# Dynamics frontier cycle19 report

Scoped investigation finished for families143–154. All twelve overview/CONTENTS entries were read; complete technical TeX readings were performed for148 and149. Family154 introduction and dynamical-input section were read, with remaining sources downloaded but not adopted. No nested workers, publication, contact, commits, or shared-file edits were used. Pinned release: `adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a`.

The strongest outcome is an explicit independently derived affine certificate for a planar polynomial family where the standard centered entropy function fails as an exterior Lyapunov function. With six rates varying arbitrarily and measurably by ±10% around `(a,b,c,d,u,v)=(2,7,7,2,1,1)`,

    x'=g, y'=−g+u−vy, g=−ax³+bx²y−cxy²+dy³,

has the global envelope

    F=min(0,x−1/16,y−x/4,x+y−1/2,10−x−y,4x+2−y,8−x).

Every active nonconstant branch increases at least `2379/40960` outside its plateau. The plateau is an invariant absorbing pentagon, with exact bounds `x∈[1/16,8]`, `y∈[1/10,42/5]`, and entry time at most `−F(x0,y0)/(2379/40960)`. This statement is proved by universal polynomial inequalities and a Carathéodory chain rule, independently of the new release. Exact rational code checks all64 rate corners, all five vertices, and the constants. The reaction realization supplied here has three reversible linkage classes; the baseline field matches [Boros–Hofbauer Section7.2](https://arxiv.org/html/1903.03071v2#S7.SS2), whose qualitative permanence was already known. No new general permanence theorem or prior-art priority is asserted.

A decisive mechanism detail is that `8−x` is geometrically redundant for the plateau but required for this global active-region proof. Removing it preserves the pentagon but makes the five-branch envelope decrease at `(100,1/10)`. Adding redundant affine branches can therefore repair a certificate's dynamics without changing its target set. This is a concrete route toward symbolic synthesis on other systems with failed smooth entropy barriers.

Full source149 reconstruction also yields a conditional general extension: assuming its arbitrary-tolerance affine approximation lemma, one common convex absorbing set works uniformly over all measurable rates in `[k_-,k_+]`, and the complex vectors may be real rather than nonnegative integers, provided kinetic exponents equal source complex coordinates. The explicit inward derivative bound is

    δ_h=2H(k_-−|R|k_+h^H)h^M,
    M=max_y ||y||1.

This general statement remains conditional on independently auditing affine Lemma2.1; its initial technical reconstruction found no gap. Source149's [Lean scope documentation](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/lean/docs/149.md) covers only the September25 initial-condition-dependent predecessor, not October5 classwise permanence. This distinction must remain in root's assessment.

Full source148 reading isolated a second reusable mechanism: finite-law hidden entropy plus small sumset size forces fair-pair capacity across finite fine-scale bands; adapted disjoint windows turn that capacity into a contradiction without an upper growth bound for the bands' endpoints. This can reopen scale arguments blocked on effective minimum separation, when they establish the exact conditional-gain interface. It supplies no uniform bit-time or observation-depth consequence.

Family154 has additional new theorem dependencies: source145 all-orders mixing, and the distinct-slopes companion's smooth triple oscillation theorem on arbitrary invertible systems. The latter already implies triple pointwise convergence without mixing. These proofs are not reproduced in154 and were not audited here. Source148's Lean comparator/main wrapper was inspected and matches the actual Hausdorff-dimension scope; no transitive Lean build was performed.

## Family 332: a constructive ambient nonlinear smoother

Input: finite stochastic `A`, stationary `π`, integer `t≥1`, real ambient
`L1(μ)` data `x_i`. Define `W` as the stationary average of squared original
distances over walk lengths `1,…,t`, and

`J_t(y)=Σ_iπ_i‖x_i−y_i‖₁²+tΣ_ijπ_i a_ij‖y_i−y_j‖₁²`.

Author claim: `J_t(y)≤3024W` for real ℓ1, using expected coordinatewise
medians of three independent endpoints from
`G=[I+t(I−A)]⁻¹`. The source combines a finite binary cut lift, cubic
boundary flattening, a quartic martingale potential, and geometric killing.

Our reconstructed improvements and consequences, with full proofs in
[MEDIAN_LIFT.md](/Users/harjas/Documents/ChatGPT/Literature Digestion/notes/frontier_functional/cycle19/MEDIAN_LIFT.md):

The independent checker reconstructed the full source assembly and all these
deductions. Its [independent note](/Users/harjas/Documents/ChatGPT/Literature Digestion/notes/frontier_check_median/cycle19/INDEPENDENT_GATE_AND_ACQUISITION.md)
also derived a more useful acquisition interface, which we independently
rechecked: `E|med₃(X)|²≤3(E|X|)²`, needing only the first absolute moment.
For `L` independent triples per row this yields

`EJ_t(ŷ)≤[18+sqrt(54(1+4t)/L)]²W`.

The confidence version replaces `L` by `Lδ` inside the square root; simplex
repair multiplies objective bounds by four. A row query needs `3L`
geometric endpoint walks, expected `3Lt` transitions, data-vector reads,
and actual coordinate/output work. Supplied sparse probability vectors make
the query independent of the ambient dimension in sample count; arithmetic
and output size remain charged. A fixed random tape gives a consistent
implicit map. This estimates the sanctioned median functional, not the
original endpoint mean or arbitrary unknown distributions in ℓ1.

## Primary technical result

On a single smooth complete metric on `R×S²`, constructed from locally finite sharp smooth dips in a sphere-radius profile,

\[
\lambda_4(-4\Delta+R)\ge2,\qquad
\lambda_a(-a\Delta+R)=-\infty\quad\text{for every }0<a<4.
\]

Any finite `k` diagonal log warps necessarily yield a positive supersolution at coefficient `4k/(k+1)`. Thus their scalar curvature cannot have any finite global lower bound. Every compact exhaustion member nevertheless admits arbitrarily close to scalar lower bound2 by splitting the logarithmic drift into sufficiently many equal warps. Stabilization rank and exhaustion infimum do not interchange.

Full proof: [FINITE_RANK_ENDPOINT_OBSTRUCTION.md](</Users/harjas/Documents/ChatGPT/Literature Digestion/notes/frontier_geometry1/cycle19/FINITE_RANK_ENDPOINT_OBSTRUCTION.md>). It specifies the smooth clipping function, the globally glued profile, properness/completeness, a global supersolution, fixed supported tests, uniform numerator estimates, divergent derivative energy, arbitrary-warps obstruction, and higher-dimensional products.

The corrected fixed-rank interface is `sup_warps inf scalar=λ_(4k/(k+1))`. A useful sufficient repair is explicit: if `R≥−C` and `λ_4≥λ>0`, any integer `k>C/λ` admits positive finite stabilization. The characterization uses standard positive-solution theory; no novelty is claimed for it alone. The obstruction's priority has not been exhaustively checked, and independent external validation remains absent.

**Incremental reusable mechanisms were reconstructed and tested; no foundational

The main mathematical output is a charged procedure combining positive
Fourier-moment repair with unconditional signed witness transfer. If c_S are
supplied exact character expectations, q=sum|c_S| and H=sum c_S chi_S, then
Q=(P+(q-H)U)/(1+q) is a probability law with zero selected moments and
TV(P,Q)<=q/(1+q). A nested witness surplus P[J intersect M]-aP[J] loses at most
TV(P,Q) under transfer. These sharpen the source's elementary bounds, but the
repair is exactly Alon-Goldreich-Mansour's2002/2003 prior construction after
flattening sequential parity mixtures. It is not a new method or headline proof.

Eight frozen exact cases passed normalization, positivity, moment, and TV checks.

tied three; clipping/normalization violated target moments twice. All eight LP
optima have independently verified exact rational primal/dual certificates.

from finite iid data cannot be silently assumed; a Bernoulli two-law calculation

or sparse progression problems.

# Families011–021: scoped frontier investigation

Worker: frontier_number2, cycle19. Assigned model policy: GPT-6.1Sol/max, no nested workers. Pinned release commit: adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a. All writes are within the assigned worker directories. No publication, contact, paid service, or repository commit.

The strongest candidate is family020's general affine determinant method for moving varieties. Its adaptive-prime deletion mechanism was reconstructed independently. The complete55-page manuscript was read; its final numerical schedule was independently checked with integer/rational arithmetic. The main squarefree-density claim remains a source theorem awaiting independent universal geometry/formal-dependency review, not an adopted programme discovery.

The best concrete downstream consequence is arithmetic-to-dynamics transfer. Conditional on the claimed exact Euler-product density, the indicator of squarefree values of every admissible fixed quartic is approximable in Cesaro L1 by periodic finite-prime sieves. Combining this with the independently proved divisibility criterion and the established Bergelson–Kułaga-Przymus–Lemańczyk–Richter theorem gives:

**R_f={n≥1:f(n) is squarefree} is an averaging set of polynomial multiple recurrence if and only if f(0) is squarefree.**

Thus n^4+1 and n^4+2 can supply parameters for polynomial configurations in every positive-upper-density set. The example n^4+12 is locally admissible and irreducible but excludes every even parameter, demonstrating why positive squarefree density does not suffice. This is a new combination/application in the present investigation, not a new general ergodic principle or an externally validated quartic theorem.

The same periodic approximation gives exact independence from every fixed vector of irrational polynomial phases satisfying Weyl's integer-combination condition. It has no asserted uniformity for shrinking Bohr sets, changing coefficients, or growing moduli. An expanded period costs exp(Θ(R)) entries; the factored local-root description costs O(DR) bits for fixed k and degree sum D. The Cesaro limit does not furnish an effective finite-prefix threshold.



## Checked mathematical extension and negative result

The release removes one global exceptional family of quintic values to sharpen its quartic line-curve bound. A tangent reconstruction extends this to any fixed d,k≥2: minimal-degree polynomials of degree M=d(k−1)+1 with d fixed distinct critical values, each critical multiplicity k−1, form a finite family after two input affine normalizations. Their integer values over a **fixed** number field cost O(X^(1/M)). Deleting this family globally improves deg b from (d−1)(k−1) to (d−1)(k−1)+1 for all d,k.

This extension yields **zero improvement** to the release's cumulative exponents in all3,989 scheduled cells of degrees4–8. Higher-dimensional maxima or the U/2 curve branch dominate. This null result prevents presenting the local refinement as a stronger power-free theorem.

The exact scan reproduced all Table1 stopping indices and gaps, and150 derivative endpoint inequalities. It executes newly written arithmetic code, not downloaded repository scripts. Seven exact finite tangent tests support the critical-data proof and identify the loss of one rank after omitting one value constraint. These finite calculations verify their scoped claims; they are not a proof of the release's universal geometry.

# Cycle19 probability bridge report

**Strongest supported result:** a complete finite strongly-Rayleigh response-to-sampler reduction with explicit total-variation error, mean-query count, and finite-bit implementation contract. Together with exact weaker-access lower bounds, it separates structural generation from acquired inference. This is incremental research capital; no consequential breakthrough or cross-family efficient acquisition theorem was established.

For `mu` on `{0,1}^n`, set `p(h)=E_{mu_h}X`. The old SR covariance row bound gives `Dp=Cov<=I/2`. After shifting Gaussian observations by `t/2`, the posterior flow is autonomous:

\[
 dH_t=(p(H_t)-\tfrac12\mathbf1)dt+dW_t.
\]

With `eta=T/K<=1`, certified coordinate mean error `zeta`, and endpoint thresholding, the independent derivation gives

\[
 \operatorname{TV}(\widehat\mu,\mu)
 \le n e^{-T/8}+\sqrt{7nT\eta/96+nT\zeta^2/2}.
\]

Choosing `T=8 log(2n/epsilon)`, `K=ceil(max(T,7nT^2/(12epsilon^2)))`, and `zeta=epsilon/(2sqrt(nT))` gives TV at most `epsilon` using `O(n log^2(n/epsilon)/epsilon^2)` complete mean queries. Mean acquisition, representation, field-dependent precision, and arithmetic costs remain explicitly charged. A separate finite-bit coupling contract supplies a stronger required numerical mean precision and polynomial random-bit count; it is not assumed free.

An independent Wiener-LSI proof gives `R(mu*Gaussian_s)<=s^2(exp(1/s)-1)` for every `s>0`. The smoothing is universally log-concave iff `s>=1/2`, with sharpness at the one-of-two SR law. The conjectured use of new093 is therefore unnecessary for this class. The response/innovation primitives are older than the release; generic SR and diffusion sampling already have primary prior art.

The exact acquisition counterexamples are:

- A hidden point mass is strongly Rayleigh with zero response, yet a blackbox point-mass value oracle requires at least `(1-epsilon)2^n-1` queries for uniform TV accuracy if no support point or sample is supplied.
- With `p=1/(1+exp(B))`, base samples cannot distinguish `delta_0` from `Bernoulli(p)` cheaply, but field `B` tilts their targets to `delta_0` and `Bernoulli(1/2)`. Adaptive stopping obeys `E_0 N >=(1/2-2epsilon)(1+exp(B))` for uniform tilted-inference accuracy. This does not imply hardness for averaged errors on typical diffusion trajectories.

221/222 Gibbs targets do not inherit SR response from their variational laws; an allowed221 positive-bond disorder event already violates pairwise negative covariance. The QAOA281 main source expressly provides no depth/finite-size modulus or efficient angle selection. Its linked SKValue formalization scope explicitly does not assert QAOA circuit-energy convergence. Thus no sampler acquisition was smuggled through these interfaces.

**Proof/verification status:** full scientific source231 read and internally reconstructed; 093/221/222/281 exact theorem/interface excerpts only, with unread decisive proofs clearly recorded. Peer `pde_gate10` independently rebuilt the finite Euler constants, LSI consequence, DPP interface and access obstructions in `notes/pde_gate10/cycle19/FINITE_RESPONSE_SAMPLER_INDEPENDENT_GATE.md`. This is internal reconstruction, not external validation. The peer's universal local-graph FUSF obstruction P3–P5 was independently checked here and saved in an owned gate note.

The final integer/rational finite-precision contract also passed the peer's G6 reconstruction, including parameter acquisition, the polynomial upper bound for path amplification, dyadic quantile approximation and endpoint anti-concentration. It remains an evaluator/quantile-service reduction with encoded input and field-magnitude costs charged; no generic evaluator acquisition was certified.

## 4. Accuracy bits and the area-law boundary

**Precision ingredient available.** The source recursion has fewer than J=32768(n+1)^3 levels, each adding at most 18(3/4)^L clean-workspace operator error. For epsilon=2^-B, set

    L=4(B+ceil log2(36J)),
    eta<=2^-B/[2(S+1)].

The skeleton contributes at most epsilon/2; slot errors contribute at most epsilon/2. Its width Q=O((n+1)^4L^2) and slot count S=O((n+1)^4L^3). Retaining the manuscript's actual Solovay--Kitaev recurrence epsilon_next<=C epsilon^(3/2), length_next<=5length gives word length O(log^4(2/xi)), since log_(3/2)5<4. The complete oracle circuit, addresses and structure generation are polynomial in n,B. The separate phase compiler preserves scalar phase, and clean error epsilon gives full diamond error at most 2epsilon. This accuracy-bit consequence was independently derived by pde_gate9, then checked here.

Q4 therefore removes a precision-cost concern within the supplied unrestricted-oracle model. It supplies no target table, physical bath controls or exact-zero Hamiltonian completeness. Detailed cost composition: `notes/pde_gate9/cycle19/BOOLEAN_UNITARY_RECONSTRUCTION_AND_PRECISION.md`, U9-U10; parameter derivation also in our Boolean note.

**265 precise scope.** The PEPS companion assumes fixed local dimension, strength, a full excitation gap and unique ground state on an open square grid. It gives polynomial bond dimension and normalized global vector error at most 1/L. Its introduction supplies the ground state Omega to the initial party; the tensors may depend arbitrarily on Omega. Its complete proof was not audited here.

The area-law positive replacement, section 3 Proposition `prop:positive`, was reconstructed: filter local h_i and subtract <Omega,h_i Omega>I to obtain M_i, then k_i=|M_i|/c*. One has k_i positive contractions, k_i Omega=0, sum_i k_i>=(H-E0)/c*, with stretched-exponential quasi-local tails exp(-cr^alpha), alpha=p/(p+1)<1. This is useful positive conversion at a supplied gapped-ground-state interface. It does not remove the imported debt:

- The full excitation gap is distinct from a normalized ground-energy promise gap. Nonlocal or degenerate hard sparse-PCP inputs are outside the theorem's hypotheses.
- Ground expectations are charged input data; no efficient centering or PEPS tensor acquisition is provided.
- At inverse-polynomial error, radius scales as log(N)^(1/alpha). A square-lattice ball has O(log(N)^(2/alpha)) sites. Its dense Hilbert-space row support q^O(log(N)^(2/alpha)) is not polynomially bounded by this result.
- There are O(volume) positive anchors. Normalizing their total operator by a worst-case extensive norm can lose an extensive gap factor. Exact annihilation may also be lost under truncation.

The same existential vectors in 277 and target tables in 283 cannot be silently reused as physical state preparations. Continuum bath preparation retains the earlier oscillator-control and finite-truncation acquisition debt. The actual general common-positive-encoding target remains OPEN.

W-rounding-spectral | rounding spectral

## 3. Exact conditional pin first moment, with two obstructions

For a random permutation Π satisfying every exterior-minor identity E∧^kΠ=∧^kK, K=EΠ, partition labels J⊔Q and condition every cycle to hit J. Exact block determinants give

    Pr(G)=det(I−K_QQ),
    E[Σ|G]=K_JJ+K_JQ(I−K_QQ)^{-1}K_QJ.

The stronger arbitrary-W determinant identity proves every entry of the next-pin mean. Independent Bernoulli transpositions satisfy the exterior premise by rank-one affinity and independence. Given rational K, one determinant and linear solve compute the conditional mean in O(n³) rational operations and polynomial bit complexity; rejection of a rare event is unnecessary.

Two valid counterexamples delimit this service. Uniform S4 and (1+χ_(2,2)/2)/24 have identical exterior moments but different identity probabilities1/24 and1/12: exterior information does not identify the law. One shared physical X flip on two spins is (12)(34); at probability1/3 it has E detΠ=1 but detEΠ=1/9, violating the premise. This refutes the literal computational-configuration transfer, not passive quantum memory generally.

Complete proof: `EXTERIOR_PIN_TRANSFER_AND_OBSTRUCTIONS.md`. Runnable exact standard-library evidence: `work/rounding_spectral/cycle19/exterior_pin_exact.py`, result `exterior_pin_exact.json`. It checks all70 minors, exact all-hit probability31/64 and the conditional2×2 mean. This finite evidence does not test either physical thermodynamic theorem. Alon–Kozma's older one-pin long-cycle formula is an inspected primary comparator, not a new result here.



## Physical claims and remaining decisive audit

The ferromagnet source claims a translation-invariant zero-field β-KMS state with ω(S₀^z)≥S/4 for fixed finite β above a threshold, d≥3. Its loop/exposure, sparse geometry, deterministic flow pairing, weighted heat-energy cancellation and positive-field KMS passage were reconstructed in the full read. The hard-sphere source claims positive constant-orbital occupation in exact canonical Gibbs states at each sufficiently dilute fixed density and a positive temperature T_phys=ρ^{3/2}a^{5/2}/(4α₀^{5/2}), held fixed before volume tends to infinity. Its purification/trajectory, spatial moment, bridge, group, route and scaling composition was reconstructed.

No counterexample to either stated physical main claim was found in that full read. These major claims still merit a second hostile reconstruction of their geometric routing/heat-flow and trajectory-trace compositions. Internal mathematical agreement, source integrity and small exact replay are not independent external validation or a priority determination. Older boundary connectivity, strong-Rayleigh preservation, exponential intersection paths and finite-range dynamics remain clearly named prerequisites with their actual read scopes.

The next high-value technical step is to acquire one concrete base-factor/target-set/collision and outside-marginal service, or a local-field response at the trace interface, with costs matching an equally informed baseline. None of the present results solves affordable interacting Gibbs preparation, passive product-bath memory or the original exact-frequency dynamics bottleneck.

N46-compact-quantum-classicality | Intrinsic classicality of uniformly compact quantum experiments
status=source_derived_unreviewed;COMPLETE supplied R evidence package;branch-specific internal proof/audit status below,external correctness/priority unresolved;checks SOURCE-REPORTED unless marked otherwise.
Notation local.

SOURCE R01 | Direct theorem candidate; independent internal audits; priority unverified
For uniformly trace-total-bounded finite-dimensional experiment sequences, vanishing optimal uniform measure-and-prepare reconstruction error, maximal pair root-fidelity minus affinity, and two-receiver marginal broadcasting error are equivalent. No common reference or domination needed. Explicit broadcasting-to-classicality bounds also permit growing entropy when b_n log N_n(a_n) tends to zero for some a_n tending to zero.
Mechanism:Canonical likelihood stability, spectral binning, tracial almost-commuting approximation, Raynaud compatible Lp ultraproduct finite-mixture lemma, established finite-label information extraction and minimax.
Limits:Mixture modulus remains qualitative. Explicit two-broadcast gap rate is O(sqrt(epsilon log(1/epsilon))), with known extraction prior art. No efficient net/channel acquisition, independent cloning or purification preservation.
Definitions T=||rho-sigma||1/2,delta=sup_pairs(||sqrt(rho)sqrt(sigma)||1-Tr sqrt(rho)sqrt(sigma)),e=inf_EB sup_E T(Lambda rho,rho),b2=inf_CPTP broadcaster sup_E,bothmarginals T. One unknown-member-independent channel on FULL algebra. Under everyaccuracy a>0 netcardinality<=N(a) independentn: e->0 iffdelta->0 iffb2->0,dimensions unrestricted;no suppliedreference/domination/Gibbs/scores. Universal e>=delta²/8. Classical alphabet atfixedtolerance independentHilbertdimension;arbitraryprescribedK_n marginalreceivers sameerror,correlations allowed,notjointindependentcopies/purification.
Canonical X=omega^-1/2(sqrt(omega)rho sqrt(omega))^1/2 omega^-1/2,X omega X=rho;rho<=C omega givesX<=sqrtC I,||X sqrtomega-sqrtrho||2²<=2sqrtC Delta(omega,rho). N103-optimal-gaussian-concentration | One-copy optimal and collective-optimal fourth-order concentration
status=source_derived_unreviewed;COMPLETE supplied Y evidence package;branch-specific internal proof/audit status below,external correctness/priority unresolved;checks SOURCE-REPORTED unless marked otherwise.
Notation local.

For standard balanced magic rho_eta, target mixed resource rho_q, 0<eta<=q<=sqrt(2)/(1+sqrt(2)). On each of 4 modes apply Gaussian filter K_s=sqrt(s)|0><0|+|1><1|, s=eta(1-q)/(q(1-eta)). Four successes yield K rho_eta K†=p_* rho_q, p_*=eta^4(1-q)²/[q^4(1-eta)²]=R_eta/R_q. It saturates robustness monotonicity,so no one-copy trace-nonincreasing branchwise C_G-preserving map has higher success. First failure projects a mode empty;both terms of magic ray no longer coexist,so failure branch is Slater mixture/free. Ancilla implementation costs up to four occupied Gaussian ancillas and four measurements per attempt;physical preparation/energy/hardware are charged,Gaussian-free only means no non-Gaussian resource.
For collective Gaussian/free-preserving processing, the converse follows from rho_eta^tensor n <=(1+R_eta)^n sigma_+^tensor n gives p R_q<=(1+R_eta)^n-1, hence n>=log(1+pR_q)/log(1+R_eta)=Omega(eta^-4) for fixed target q>0 and constant p;repeat independent filters attains Theta(eta^-4). Approx output epsilon uses max(R_q-2epsilon,0) witness. Example eta=.1 to q=.5 gives p=1/2025,2025 mean independent attempts. This is fixed mixed-resource concentration,not pure-state distillation,QEC/fault-tolerant achievability,or experiment. Written proof and 20 dense parameter pairs/80 failure branches/four symbolic identities source-reported;not replayed.

N104-signed-gaussian-estimation | Signed Gaussian estimator for bounded outputs; no positive-sampler transfer
status=source_derived_unreviewed;COMPLETE supplied Y evidence package;branch-specific internal proof/audit status below,external correctness/priority unresolved;checks SOURCE-REPORTED unless marked otherwise.
Notation local.

For standard magic blocks with exact decompositions rho_i=(1+R_i)sigma_i^+-R_i sigma_i^-, convex-Gaussian terms,independent signs and Gaussian output sampler,absolute mass Gamma=prod_i(1+2R_i). For any efficiently computable |f(X)|<=1, signed estimator Y=Gamma*s*f(X) is unbiased and |Y|<=Gamma;Hoeffding sample cost O(Gamma² epsilon^-2 log(2/alpha)) for additive epsilon,failure alpha. With eta_i bounded away from1 and sum eta_i^4=O(log M), cost polynomial,including inverse-polynomial additive accuracy. This logarithmic regime is stronger for ESTIMATION than positive mixture's full-law condition n eta^4=O(epsilon).
Do not infer a full positive sampler: signed weights aren't probabilities,rare conditional probabilities can require exponential relative accuracy. Actual code/Monte Carlo is floating-point; source report logs rounding exclusion and a repaired diagnostic branch clipping issue,so stated exact-arithmetic estimator proof is separate from uncertified software execution.
